- **issue-tracker**: Issues and specs for this repo live as GitHub issues. Use the `gh` CLI for all operations. In a cloud lane, `gh issue`, `gh pr` and `gh project` fail (they use GraphQL, which the cloud proxy refuses): use `gh api` over REST there. `docs/agents/github-rest.md` lists the equivalents, and the scripts below already use them.
